# Brigitte's sounds: <sound id> = <file under E:/Overwatch Archive/extracted/154088/> (build 154088). Same format as
# sombra.txt / ana.txt; build_asset_pack.py sounds copies / mixes them into the local pack. Nothing here has been
# listened to. Read 2026-11-01 offline (Overwatch and Battle.net checked closed before every archive read and DataTool /
# TankLib run): her 02C sounds' Wwise banks decoded exactly (ww.py, dump_banks.py, emote_events.py / wwfull.py from
# research/emote-sounds/scripts: children from each object's parent id, random / sequence / switch / layer parameters,
# volume, make-up gain, pitch, delays, the events' actions, RTPC curves), the Init bank's bus volumes and game parameter
# defaults (1E818, stmg.py). Scripts: E:/Overwatch Archive/research/brigitte/scripts/sounds/ (brigitte_gen.py writes this
# file; Sombra's pipeline copied and retargeted, her scripts unchanged); their data (decode brigitte_ww.json, bank dump,
# measurements, the effects her graphs reach by override fx_proj.json, contact sets contacts.json / contacts_whip.json,
# voice lines, the RTPC defaults): research/brigitte/sounds/. Effect and clip timings: research/brigitte/work/
# fx_sounds_timed.json (fx_sounds2_brigitte.py over work/graph_effects.json); graph nodes: research/brigitte/work_kit
# decompiles (fx_nodes.py) and kit_*.md; the kit itself: tools/reference/brigitte-map.md.
# The live kit: Rocket Flail (and quick melee, the same swing), Whip Shot, Repair Pack, Barrier Shield, Shield Bash,
# Inspire, Rally and the stuns it gives; two of her live perks as EXTRA (Whiplash, Inspiring Strike). The Stadium powers
# (Shield Throw, charges), the Story Mission copies (463A / 463B: the same effects) are left out ('Not mapped').
# Files: her skin and voice extraction are in research/brigitte/extract, not under the build root, so those paths reach it
# through ../../research/brigitte/extract/ (brigitte_gen.py --canonical writes Heroes/Brigitte/... and HeroVoice/Brigitte/...);
# the 19 banks it lacked were extracted for this map into ExtraSounds/Brigitte (extract_banks.py, DataTool's own sound
# export; manifest.json there: the flail's contacts (FlailContact), Whip Shot's (WhipShotContact), the flail head's own
# set (WhipContact, not built)). Ids: 02C sounds (5E53 = 000000005E53.02C), 00D effects, 006 clips, 08F clip animation
# effects, 049 contact sets; graphs 01B with their node numbers. Confidence: H read in the data, M inferred, L guess.
# Gains: Wwise volume + make-up gain summed from each file up through its containers and mixers (the middle of a random
# range included) plus its output bus chain, plus RTPC curves (dB scaling 20*log10(1+y); linear between points) and
# SetVolume actions. RTPCs are read at their game parameter's default (the Init bank's list); modulators: an LFO at its
# middle, a time modulator at its start on one-shots and at its end on loops. As in sombra.txt, ONE offset per family
# keeps her sounds' balance with each other, bringing the family's loudest single layer to -1 dBFS (the builder's limiter
# catches sums): own -1.5 dB (loudest: brigitte.whip.hit), 3p -3 dB (loudest: brigitte.whip.hit_3p).
# Pitch: cents as a speed factor (*). Low / high-pass filters, random pitch / volume per play, fades, Seek, Wwise states
# and the voice priority RTPCs are not reproduced. Layers more than 60 dB down are left out, and in loops those 20 dB
# under the loudest loop. A loop's LOOP LENGTH is its longest bed within 6 dB of the loudest layer; shorter layers are
# tiled across it and beds more than 15 % longer are left out.
# Own vs others: her STUSounds with audience 1 (5E53, 5E52, 46FA, 62CF, 476F, 4772, 5E4C, 4742, 6347, 4779, 5A06-5A08, 4690,
# 4691, 5E4E, 4686, 4688, 5E4F) are read as hers (H by Lucio's pairs: audience 1 was his own, 2 the others'); 4691 carries a
# range because it sounds at the healed ally. The other no-range copies (audience 2) are read as the player the effect
# plays on (M, Ana's and Sombra's reading): 'own' is Brigitte, or the healed / inspired / overhealed ally or the stunned /
# slammed target where a rule says. Ranged copies are everyone else's ('3p'); where an effect or contact holds two ranged
# copies with the same files they are read as the two teams' (or near / far) copies (L which): the louder is built, the
# other noted; copies with different files all play (built together, M). Contact sounds with no own copy are built once for
# everyone ('both'). Ranges come through the volume (sounds.md). Her swing rules hold the clip and effect offsets of one
# swing: play them at the swing's start.

# ===== How each game event is put together (rules)
# brigitte.flail.forehand: forehand, own: the 1P clip's foley 476F (no range, audience 1: a random 8 of 0.33-0.42 s at
#  +300 cents, a random 8 of 0.21-0.35 s 0.2 s late, a random 10 of 0.07-0.18 s 0.3 s late) and 219B's swing 5E53 (no
#  range, audience 1: a random 3 of 0.68 s whoosh, a random 8 of 0.23-0.35 s at +312 cents, a 0.41 s file at -450
#  cents).
# Trigger: a forehand swing starts (PRIMARY or quick melee held; combo index v59 0 or 2, 1248 #807 -> #345): the 1P
#  forehand clip 4F53 (cat 0x6F, from 0.15 s, #607 -> #560; effect 2624.08F plays 476F at 0.033 s) and effect 219B as
#  the 0.4 s recovery starts at 0.2 s (#353 StateChaseVar -> #693, H); this event is played at the swing's start and
#  holds both offsets.
# Layers: a 476F 322862679 RanSeq 0.07-0.18 s x10 +75 cents, net -7.5; b 476F 740731944 RanSeq 0.33-0.42 s x8 +300
#  cents, net -4; c 5E53 692485220 RanSeq 0.23-0.35 s x8 +312 cents, net -9.5; d 476F 919157232 RanSeq 0.21-0.35 s x8
#  -40 cents, net -9; e 5E53 678229984 RanSeq 0.68-0.69 s x3, net -2.5; f 5E53 174548184 Sound 0.41 s -450 cents, net
#  -16.
@brigitte.flail.forehand = brigitte.flail.forehand.a*1.044@0.483:-9 + brigitte.flail.forehand.b*1.189@0.183:-5.5 + brigitte.flail.forehand.c*1.197@0.23:-11 + brigitte.flail.forehand.d*0.977@0.383:-10.5 + brigitte.flail.forehand.e@0.2:-4 + brigitte.flail.forehand.f*0.771@0.2:-17.5
# brigitte.flail.forehand_3p: forehand heard by others: 4770 (20 m: a random 6 of 0.75-0.86 s) + 76A0 (60 m: 5E53's
#  whoosh and +312 set, 11 dB quieter, no -450 file).
# Trigger: the same forehand, at her for everyone else: the 3P clip 4E9E (from 0.15 s; effect 26EB.08F plays 4770 at
#  0) and 219B's 60 m copy 76A0 at 0.2 s.
# Layers: a 76A0 1051779257 RanSeq 0.23-0.35 s x8 +312 cents, net -20.5; b 4770 695539017 RanSeq 0.75-0.86 s x6, net
#  -30.5; c 76A0 926873390 RanSeq 0.68-0.69 s x3, net -13.5.
@brigitte.flail.forehand_3p = brigitte.flail.forehand_3p.a*1.197@0.23:-23.5 + brigitte.flail.forehand_3p.b@0.15:-33.5 + brigitte.flail.forehand_3p.c@0.2:-16.5
# brigitte.flail.backhand: backhand, own: 219C's 5E52 (no range, audience 1: a random 3 of 0.67-0.75 s whoosh 0.15 s
#  late, the +312 set 0.205 s late, the -450 file) and the 1P clip's foley 476F.
# Trigger: a backhand swing starts (combo index v59 1, 1248 #808 -> #346): effect 219C at once (#649 StateChaseVar ->
#  #692) and the 1P backhand clip 4F52 (cat 0x70, from 0.2 s, #608 -> #561; effect 262C.08F plays 476F at 0.033 s)
#  (H).
# Layers: a 476F 322862679 RanSeq 0.07-0.18 s x10 +75 cents, net -7.5; b 5E52 692485220 RanSeq 0.23-0.35 s x8 +312
#  cents, net -9.5; c 476F 740731944 RanSeq 0.33-0.42 s x8 +300 cents, net -4; d 476F 919157232 RanSeq 0.21-0.35 s x8
#  -40 cents, net -9; e 5E52 663886804 RanSeq 0.67-0.75 s x3, net -3.5; f 5E52 174548184 Sound 0.41 s -450 cents, net
#  -16.
@brigitte.flail.backhand = brigitte.flail.backhand.a*1.044@0.533:-9 + brigitte.flail.backhand.b*1.197@0.205:-11 + brigitte.flail.backhand.c*1.189@0.233:-5.5 + brigitte.flail.backhand.d*0.977@0.433:-10.5 + brigitte.flail.backhand.e@0.15:-5 + brigitte.flail.backhand.f*0.771:-17.5
# brigitte.flail.backhand_3p: backhand heard by others: 769D (60 m: 5E52's whoosh and +312 set, 11 dB quieter) + 4770 (20 m).
# Trigger: the same backhand, at her for everyone else: 219C's 60 m copy 769D at once and the 3P clip 4E9F (from 0.2
#  s; 27CC.08F plays 4770 at 0).
# Layers: a 769D 1051779257 RanSeq 0.23-0.35 s x8 +312 cents, net -20.5; b 4770 695539017 RanSeq 0.75-0.86 s x6, net
#  -30.5; c 769D 152644580 RanSeq 0.67-0.75 s x3, net -14.5.
@brigitte.flail.backhand_3p = brigitte.flail.backhand_3p.a*1.197@0.205:-23.5 + brigitte.flail.backhand_3p.b@0.2:-33.5 + brigitte.flail.backhand_3p.c@0.15:-17.5
# brigitte.flail.release: flail put up, own: 223A's 46FA (no range, audience 1: a random 6 of 0.55 s at +50 cents) and
#  the 1P clip's foley 4772 (0.33-0.42 s at +300 0.2 s late, 0.21-0.35 s 0.2 s late, 0.07-0.18 s 0.3 s late).
# Trigger: PRIMARY is let go after a swing (the recovery anim: 1248 #588 alias 0x112 0.7 s after combo index 0 / 2,
#  #591 0x113 0.63 s after 1; both onBegin -> #648 ActionEffect 223A, H) and the 1P release clips 4F37 (0x72: 2632.08F
#  plays 4772 at 0.033 s) / 4F55 (0x73: at 0) (H; one rule for both, M).
# Layers: a 4772 99230828 RanSeq 0.07-0.18 s x10 +75 cents, net -7.5; b 4772 387335684 RanSeq 0.33-0.42 s x8 +300
#  cents, net -7; c 4772 657131097 RanSeq 0.21-0.35 s x8 -40 cents, net -9; d 46FA 655677647 RanSeq 0.55 s x6 +50
#  cents, net -16.5.
@brigitte.flail.release = brigitte.flail.release.a*1.044@0.333:-9 + brigitte.flail.release.b*1.189@0.233:-8.5 + brigitte.flail.release.c*0.977@0.233:-10.5 + brigitte.flail.release.d*1.029:-18

# brigitte.flail.hit: flail hit on an enemy, own: 87F6 (no range, audience 2: a random 17 of 0.58-0.79 s, a random 10

#  as hers, the hit confirm (M: the no-range copy of a contact). Not in her extraction

#  effect 29A9 -> 87F6 + 902D + 9015 (H data; M that type 0 is the character contact: its materials 008D / 008E / 00EC

# brigitte.repair.cast: Repair Pack arm foley, own: 4779 (no range, audience 1: six random sets of 0.07-0.42 s, three
#  at once and three 0.65-0.7 s late).
# Trigger: the Repair Pack cast (ABILITY 2 on a target, 0EDD #321; anims 0505 0.57 s + 04AA 1.0 s): the 1P clip 4F5A
#  (0x505, 0.967 s; 27DA.08F plays 4779 at 0.023 s, H); the 3P clip 5062 carries no sound (H).
# Layers: a 4779 546981851 RanSeq 0.07-0.18 s x10 +75 cents, net -10.5; b 4779 475128963 RanSeq 0.33-0.42 s x8 +500
